# Rezultati merenja

### bez Retriever

| ID | Pitanje | Tačno | Samoispr. | SQL koji je sistem izvršio | Greška |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| count_1 | Koliko ima filmova u bazi? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film LIMIT 100` |  |
| count_2 | Koliko ima glumaca? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM actor LIMIT 100` |  |
| count_3 | Koliko ima kupaca? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM customer LIMIT 100` |  |
| count_4 | Koliko razlicitih jezika postoji u bazi? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT language_id) FROM language LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_1 | Koji filmovi traju duze od 180 minuta? Prikazi naziv. | DA | 0 | `SELECT title FROM film WHERE length > 180 LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_2 | Koliko filmova ima ocenu 'R'? | DA | 1 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film WHERE rating = 'R' LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_3 | Koliko filmova ima cenu iznajmljivanja vecu od 4? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film WHERE rental_rate > 4 LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_4 | Prikazi ime i prezime kupaca cije prezime pocinje na slovo 'B'. | DA | 0 | `SELECT first_name, last_name FROM customer WHERE last_name LIKE 'B%' LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_5 | Koliko filmova ima trajanje izmedju 60 i 90 minuta? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film WHERE length BETWEEN 60 AND 90 LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_1 | Koja je prosecna duzina filma? | DA | 0 | `SELECT AVG(length) AS average_length FROM film LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_2 | Koliki je ukupan prihod od svih placanja? | DA | 0 | `SELECT SUM(amount) FROM payment LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_3 | Koja je najveca, a koja najmanja cena iznajmljivanja filma? | DA | 0 | `SELECT MAX(rental_rate) AS max_rental_rate, MIN(rental_rate) AS min_rental_rate FROM film LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_4 | Koliko filmova ima u svakoj kategoriji? Prikazi kategoriju i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.name AS category_name, COUNT(fc.film_id) AS film_count FROM film_category AS fc JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id GROUP BY c.category_id, c.name LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_5 | Koliko kupaca je registrovano u svakoj prodavnici? Prikazi store_id i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT s.store_id, COUNT(c.customer_id) AS customer_count FROM store AS s JOIN customer AS c ON s.store_id = c.store_id GROUP BY s.store_id LIMIT 100` |  |
| topn_1 | Kojih deset filmova je najduze? Prikazi naziv i duzinu. | DA | 0 | `SELECT title, length FROM film ORDER BY length DESC LIMIT 10` |  |
| topn_2 | Koji glumac glumi u najvise filmova? Prikazi ime, prezime i broj filmova. | DA | 0 | `SELECT a.first_name, a.last_name, COUNT(fa.film_id) AS film_count FROM actor AS a JOIN film_actor AS fa ON a.actor_id = fa.actor_id GROUP BY a.actor_id ORDER BY film_count DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| topn_3 | Koje tri kategorije imaju najvise filmova? Prikazi kategoriju i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.name AS category_name, COUNT(fc.film_id) AS film_count FROM film_category AS fc JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id GROUP BY c.category_id, c.name ORDER BY film_count DESC LIMIT 3` |  |
| topn_4 | Koji kupac je potrosio najvise novca? Prikazi ime, prezime i ukupan iznos. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.first_name, c.last_name, SUM(p.amount) AS total_spent FROM customer AS c JOIN payment AS p ON c.customer_id = p.customer_id GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.first_name, c.last_name ORDER BY total_spent DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| topn_5 | Iz koje drzave ima najvise kupaca? Prikazi drzavu i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT co.country AS country_name, COUNT(c.customer_id) AS customer_count FROM customer AS c JOIN address AS a ON c.address_id = a.address_id JOIN city AS ci ON a.city_id = ci.city_id JOIN country AS co ON ci.country_id = co.country_id GROUP BY co.country ORDER BY customer_count DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| join_1 | Kojih pet kupaca je obavilo najvise iznajmljivanja? Prikazi ime, prezime i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.first_name, c.last_name, COUNT(r.rental_id) AS rental_count FROM customer AS c JOIN rental AS r ON c.customer_id = r.customer_id GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.first_name, c.last_name ORDER BY rental_count DESC LIMIT 5` |  |
| join_2 | Koje filmove glumi glumac sa imenom 'NICK' i prezimenom 'WAHLBERG'? Prikazi nazive. | DA | 0 | `SELECT f.title FROM actor AS a JOIN film_actor AS fa ON a.actor_id = fa.actor_id JOIN film AS f ON fa.film_id = f.film_id WHERE a.first_name = 'NICK' AND a.last_name = 'WAHLBERG' LIMIT 100` |  |
| join_3 | Prikazi naziv filma i njegovu kategoriju za sve filmove ciji naziv pocinje slovom 'Z'. | DA | 1 | `SELECT f.title, c.name AS category FROM film AS f JOIN film_category AS fc ON f.film_id = fc.film_id JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id WHERE f.title LIKE 'Z%' LIMIT 100` |  |
| join_4 | Koliko filmova je u inventaru svake prodavnice? Prikazi store_id i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT s.store_id, COUNT(i.inventory_id) AS film_count FROM store AS s JOIN inventory AS i ON s.store_id = i.store_id GROUP BY s.store_id LIMIT 100` |  |
| join_5 | Koliko filmova je u kategoriji 'Comedy'? | DA | 2 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film AS f JOIN film_category AS fc ON f.film_id = fc.film_id JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id WHERE c.name = 'Comedy' LIMIT 100` |  |

### Pun sistem

| ID | Pitanje | Tačno | Samoispr. | SQL koji je sistem izvršio | Greška |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| hard_1 | Koji glumci se pojavljuju u vise od 35 filmova? Prikazi ime, prezime i broj filmova. | DA | 0 | `SELECT a.first_name, a.last_name, COUNT(fa.film_id) AS film_count FROM actor AS a JOIN film_actor AS fa ON a.actor_id = fa.actor_id GROUP BY a.actor_id HAVING COUNT(fa.film_id) > 35 LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_2 | Koja kategorija filmova donosi najveci ukupan prihod od placanja? | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.name, SUM(p.amount) AS total_revenue FROM category AS c JOIN film_category AS fc ON c.category_id = fc.category_id JOIN film AS f ON fc.film_id = f.film_id JOIN inventory AS i ON f.film_id = i.film_id JOIN rental AS r ON i.inventory_id = r.inventory_id JOIN payment AS p ON r.rental_id = p.rental_id GROUP BY c.category_id, c.name ORDER BY total_revenue DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| hard_3 | Koliko kupaca je potrosilo vise od prosecne potrosnje po kupcu? | NE | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT c.customer_id) FROM customer AS c JOIN payment AS p ON c.customer_id = p.customer_id GROUP BY c.customer_id HAVING SUM(p.amount) > ( SELECT AVG(total_spent) FROM ( SELECT SUM(amount) AS total_spent FROM payment GROUP BY customer_id ) AS avg_spending ) LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_4 | Koji glumac se pojavljuje u najvise filmova kategorije 'Sports'? Prikazi ime, prezime i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT a.first_name, a.last_name, COUNT(fa.film_id) AS film_count FROM actor AS a JOIN film_actor AS fa ON a.actor_id = fa.actor_id JOIN film_category AS fc ON fa.film_id = fc.film_id JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id WHERE c.name = 'Sports' GROUP BY a.actor_id ORDER BY film_count DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| hard_5 | U kojim gradovima zivi vise od jednog kupca? Prikazi grad i broj kupaca. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.city, COUNT(cs.customer_id) AS customer_count FROM customer AS cs JOIN address AS a ON cs.address_id = a.address_id JOIN city AS c ON a.city_id = c.city_id GROUP BY c.city HAVING COUNT(cs.customer_id) > 1 LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_6 | Koliko filmova nikada nije iznajmljeno? | NE | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film WHERE NOT film_id IN ( SELECT DISTINCT film_id FROM rental ) LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_7 | Koje kategorije imaju prosecnu duzinu filma vecu od ukupne prosecne duzine svih filmova? Prikazi kategoriju i prosecnu duzinu. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.name AS category, AVG(f.length) AS average_length FROM film AS f JOIN film_category AS fc ON f.film_id = fc.film_id JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id GROUP BY c.category_id HAVING AVG(f.length) > ( SELECT AVG(length) FROM film ) LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_8 | Iz koje drzave dolazi kupac koji je potrosio najvise novca? Prikazi drzavu. | NE | 0 | `SELECT co.country FROM customer AS c JOIN address AS a ON c.address_id = a.address_id JOIN city AS ci ON a.city_id = ci.city_id JOIN country AS co ON ci.country_id = co.country_id JOIN payment AS p ON c.customer_id = p.customer_id GROUP BY co.country ORDER BY SUM(p.amount) DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| hard_9 | Za svaku ocenu (rating), koliko filmova je duze od 120 minuta? Prikazi ocenu i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT f.rating, COUNT(f.film_id) AS film_count FROM film AS f WHERE f.length > 120 GROUP BY f.rating ORDER BY f.rating LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_10 | Koliko filmova se nalazi u inventaru obe prodavnice? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(film_id) AS total_films FROM ( SELECT film_id FROM inventory GROUP BY film_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT store_id) = 2 ) AS subquery LIMIT 100` |  |

| hard_1 | Koji glumci se pojavljuju u vise od 35 filmova? Prikazi ime, prezime i broj filmova. | DA | 0 | `SELECT a.first_name, a.last_name, COUNT(fa.film_id) AS film_count FROM actor AS a JOIN film_actor AS fa ON a.actor_id = fa.actor_id GROUP BY a.actor_id HAVING COUNT(fa.film_id) > 35 LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_2 | Koja kategorija filmova donosi najveci ukupan prihod od placanja? | DA | 1 | `SELECT c.name, SUM(p.amount) AS total_revenue FROM category AS c JOIN film_category AS fc ON c.category_id = fc.category_id JOIN film AS f ON fc.film_id = f.film_id JOIN inventory AS i ON f.film_id = i.film_id JOIN rental AS r ON i.inventory_id = r.inventory_id JOIN payment AS p ON r.rental_id = p.rental_id GROUP BY c.category_id, c.name ORDER BY total_revenue DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| hard_3 | Koliko kupaca je potrosilo vise od prosecne potrosnje po kupcu? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ( SELECT customer_id FROM payment GROUP BY customer_id HAVING SUM(amount) > ( SELECT AVG(total_spent) FROM ( SELECT SUM(amount) AS total_spent FROM payment GROUP BY customer_id ) AS avg_spending ) ) AS high_spenders LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_4 | Koji glumac se pojavljuje u najvise filmova kategorije 'Sports'? Prikazi ime, prezime i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT a.first_name, a.last_name, COUNT(fa.film_id) AS film_count FROM actor AS a JOIN film_actor AS fa ON a.actor_id = fa.actor_id JOIN film_category AS fc ON fa.film_id = fc.film_id JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id WHERE c.name = 'Sports' GROUP BY a.actor_id ORDER BY film_count DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| hard_5 | U kojim gradovima zivi vise od jednog kupca? Prikazi grad i broj kupaca. | NE | 2 | `SELECT a.city, COUNT(c.customer_id) AS customer_count FROM customer AS c JOIN address AS a ON c.address_id = a.address_id GROUP BY a.city HAVING COUNT(c.customer_id) > 1 LIMIT 100` | Nisam uspeo da sastavim ispravan upit za ovo pitanje. Pokušaj da ga preformulišeš jasnije ili jednostavnije. |
| hard_6 | Koliko filmova nikada nije iznajmljeno? | NE | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film WHERE NOT film_id IN ( SELECT DISTINCT film_id FROM rental ) LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_7 | Koje kategorije imaju prosecnu duzinu filma vecu od ukupne prosecne duzine svih filmova? Prikazi kategoriju i prosecnu duzinu. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.name AS category, AVG(f.length) AS average_length FROM film AS f JOIN film_category AS fc ON f.film_id = fc.film_id JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id GROUP BY c.category_id HAVING AVG(f.length) > ( SELECT AVG(length) FROM film ) LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_8 | Iz koje drzave dolazi kupac koji je potrosio najvise novca? Prikazi drzavu. | DA | 1 | `SELECT co.country AS country FROM customer AS cu JOIN address AS a ON cu.address_id = a.address_id JOIN city AS ci ON a.city_id = ci.city_id JOIN country AS co ON ci.country_id = co.country_id JOIN ( SELECT customer_id, SUM(amount) AS total_spent FROM payment GROUP BY customer_id ) AS spending ON cu.customer_id = spending.customer_id ORDER BY spending.total_spent DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| hard_9 | Za svaku ocenu (rating), koliko filmova je duze od 120 minuta? Prikazi ocenu i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT f.rating, COUNT(f.film_id) AS film_count FROM film AS f WHERE f.length > 120 GROUP BY f.rating LIMIT 100` |  |
| hard_10 | Koliko filmova se nalazi u inventaru obe prodavnice? | NE | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT i.film_id) FROM inventory AS i WHERE i.store_id IN (1, 2) GROUP BY i.film_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT i.store_id) = 2 LIMIT 100` |  |

| count_1 | Koliko ima filmova u bazi? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film LIMIT 100` |  |
| count_2 | Koliko ima glumaca? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM actor LIMIT 100` |  |
| count_3 | Koliko ima kupaca? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM customer LIMIT 100` |  |
| count_4 | Koliko razlicitih jezika postoji u bazi? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT name) FROM language LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_1 | Koji filmovi traju duze od 180 minuta? Prikazi naziv. | DA | 0 | `SELECT title FROM film WHERE length > 180 LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_2 | Koliko filmova ima ocenu 'R'? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film WHERE rating = 'R' LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_3 | Koliko filmova ima cenu iznajmljivanja vecu od 4? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film WHERE rental_rate > 4 LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_4 | Prikazi ime i prezime kupaca cije prezime pocinje na slovo 'B'. | DA | 0 | `SELECT first_name, last_name FROM customer WHERE last_name LIKE 'B%' LIMIT 100` |  |
| filter_5 | Koliko filmova ima trajanje izmedju 60 i 90 minuta? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film WHERE length BETWEEN 60 AND 90 LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_1 | Koja je prosecna duzina filma? | DA | 0 | `SELECT AVG(length) FROM film LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_2 | Koliki je ukupan prihod od svih placanja? | DA | 0 | `SELECT SUM(amount) AS total_revenue FROM payment LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_3 | Koja je najveca, a koja najmanja cena iznajmljivanja filma? | DA | 0 | `SELECT MAX(rental_rate) AS max_rental_rate, MIN(rental_rate) AS min_rental_rate FROM film LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_4 | Koliko filmova ima u svakoj kategoriji? Prikazi kategoriju i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.name AS category, COUNT(fc.film_id) AS film_count FROM film_category AS fc JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id GROUP BY c.category_id, c.name LIMIT 100` |  |
| agg_5 | Koliko kupaca je registrovano u svakoj prodavnici? Prikazi store_id i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.store_id, COUNT(c.customer_id) AS customer_count FROM customer AS c GROUP BY c.store_id LIMIT 100` |  |
| topn_1 | Kojih deset filmova je najduze? Prikazi naziv i duzinu. | DA | 0 | `SELECT title, length FROM film ORDER BY length DESC LIMIT 10` |  |
| topn_2 | Koji glumac glumi u najvise filmova? Prikazi ime, prezime i broj filmova. | DA | 0 | `SELECT a.first_name, a.last_name, COUNT(fa.film_id) AS film_count FROM actor AS a JOIN film_actor AS fa ON a.actor_id = fa.actor_id GROUP BY a.actor_id ORDER BY film_count DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| topn_3 | Koje tri kategorije imaju najvise filmova? Prikazi kategoriju i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.name AS category, COUNT(fc.film_id) AS film_count FROM film_category AS fc JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id GROUP BY c.category_id, c.name ORDER BY film_count DESC LIMIT 3` |  |
| topn_4 | Koji kupac je potrosio najvise novca? Prikazi ime, prezime i ukupan iznos. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.first_name, c.last_name, SUM(p.amount) AS total_spent FROM customer AS c JOIN payment AS p ON c.customer_id = p.customer_id GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.first_name, c.last_name ORDER BY total_spent DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| topn_5 | Iz koje drzave ima najvise kupaca? Prikazi drzavu i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT co.country AS country, COUNT(c.customer_id) AS customer_count FROM customer AS c JOIN address AS a ON c.address_id = a.address_id JOIN city AS ci ON a.city_id = ci.city_id JOIN country AS co ON ci.country_id = co.country_id GROUP BY co.country_id, co.country ORDER BY customer_count DESC LIMIT 1` |  |
| join_1 | Kojih pet kupaca je obavilo najvise iznajmljivanja? Prikazi ime, prezime i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT c.first_name, c.last_name, COUNT(r.rental_id) AS rental_count FROM customer AS c JOIN rental AS r ON c.customer_id = r.customer_id GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.first_name, c.last_name ORDER BY rental_count DESC LIMIT 5` |  |
| join_2 | Koje filmove glumi glumac sa imenom 'NICK' i prezimenom 'WAHLBERG'? Prikazi nazive. | DA | 0 | `SELECT f.title FROM actor AS a JOIN film_actor AS fa ON a.actor_id = fa.actor_id JOIN film AS f ON fa.film_id = f.film_id WHERE a.first_name = 'NICK' AND a.last_name = 'WAHLBERG' LIMIT 100` |  |
| join_3 | Prikazi naziv filma i njegovu kategoriju za sve filmove ciji naziv pocinje slovom 'Z'. | DA | 0 | `SELECT f.title, c.name FROM film AS f JOIN film_category AS fc ON f.film_id = fc.film_id JOIN category AS c ON fc.category_id = c.category_id WHERE f.title LIKE 'Z%' LIMIT 100` |  |
| join_4 | Koliko filmova je u inventaru svake prodavnice? Prikazi store_id i broj. | DA | 0 | `SELECT s.store_id, COUNT(i.inventory_id) AS film_count FROM store AS s JOIN inventory AS i ON s.store_id = i.store_id GROUP BY s.store_id LIMIT 100` |  |
| join_5 | Koliko filmova je u kategoriji 'Comedy'? | DA | 0 | `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM film WHERE film_id IN ( SELECT film_id FROM film_category WHERE category_id = ( SELECT category_id FROM category WHERE name = 'Comedy' ) ) LIMIT 100` |  |
